```ts
let globalCache: { at: number; value: { mode: ChatMode; reason?: ChatModeReason; until?: number } } | null = null;
async function cachedGlobalMode(env: ChatEnv) {
  if (globalCache && Date.now() - globalCache.at < 30_000) return globalCache.value;
  const value = await modStub(env).getGlobalMode();
  globalCache = { at: Date.now(), value };
  return value;
}
export const modStub = (env: ChatEnv) =>
  (env.CHAT_MOD as DurableObjectNamespace<ChatMod>).get(env.CHAT_MOD.idFromName('global'));
export async function identityFromCookie(request: Request, env: ChatEnv) {
  const token = readCookie(request, SESSION_COOKIE);
  if (!token) return null;
  const s = await verifySession(token, requireSecret(env.CHAT_SESSION_KEY, 'CHAT_SESSION_KEY'), Date.now());
  if (!s) return null;
  const hmac = await uidHmac(requireSecret(env.CHAT_BAN_KEY, 'CHAT_BAN_KEY'), s.uid);
  if ((await modStub(env).getGen(hmac)) !== s.gen) return null;          // revoked by ban or "log out everywhere"
  const ghId = s.uid.startsWith('gh:') ? s.uid.slice(3) : '';
  const admins = (env.CHAT_ADMIN_IDS ?? '').split(',').map((x) => x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  return { uid: s.uid, handle: s.handle, uidHmac: hmac, moderator: ghId !== '' && admins.includes(ghId) };
}
export function resetGlobalCacheForTests() { globalCache = null; }
```

The fake-env tests of Task 7 now need a `CHAT_MOD` whose stub has `getGlobalMode: async () => ({ mode: 'open' })` and `getGen: async () => 0`; update `fakeEnv` accordingly, and add a test: global mode `closed` → 503 `{ error: 'closed' }` without `CHAT_ROOM.idFromName` (call `resetGlobalCacheForTests()` in a `beforeEach`).

- [ ] **13.5 Room — connection, state and hello** (`worker/chat/room.ts`, first half):

```ts
import { DurableObject } from 'cloudflare:workers';
import { encode, parseClientFrame, MAX_FRAME_BYTES, type ClientFrame, type ServerFrame } from '../../src/lib/community/protocol';
import type { ChatMessage, ChatMode, ChatModeReason, HoldCode } from '../../src/lib/community/types';
import { applySchema, ROOM_SCHEMA } from './schema';
import { evaluate, FiltersUnavailable, type TermList } from './filters';
import { normalize } from './normalize';
import { checkIdentity, checkReport, msUntilUtcMidnight, roomBudget, roomMinute, utcDay, utcHour } from './ratelimit';
import { HISTORY_LIMIT, LIMITS } from './limits';
import type { ChatEnv } from './env';
import type { ChatMod } from './mod';

interface Conn { uid?: string; handle?: string; uidHmac?: string; moderator: boolean; rejects: number }
interface ModeState { mode: ChatMode; reason?: ChatModeReason; until?: number }
const RANK: Record<ChatMode, number> = { open: 0, slow: 1, readonly: 2, closed: 3 };

export class ChatRoom extends DurableObject<ChatEnv> {
  private terms: { list: TermList | null; at: number } | null = null;   // memory only; reloaded after hibernation
  constructor(ctx: DurableObjectState, env: ChatEnv) { super(ctx, env); applySchema(ctx.storage.sql, ROOM_SCHEMA); }

  private sql(q: string, ...b: unknown[]) { return this.ctx.storage.sql.exec(q, ...b).toArray(); }
  private kv(k: string): string | null { const r = this.sql('SELECT v FROM room WHERE k = ?', k)[0]; return r ? String(r.v) : null; }
  private setKv(k: string, v: string) { this.sql('INSERT OR REPLACE INTO room (k, v) VALUES (?, ?)', k, v); }
  private count(key: string, now = Date.now()): number {
    const r = this.sql('SELECT n FROM counters WHERE day = ? AND key = ?', utcDay(now), key)[0];
    return r ? Number(r.n) : 0;
  }
  private bump(key: string, now = Date.now(), by = 1): number {
    this.sql('INSERT INTO counters (day, key, n) VALUES (?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT(day, key) DO UPDATE SET n = n + ?', utcDay(now), key, by, by);
    return this.count(key, now);
  }
  private setCounter(key: string, n: number, now = Date.now()) {
    this.sql('INSERT OR REPLACE INTO counters (day, key, n) VALUES (?, ?, ?)', utcDay(now), key, n);
  }
  private mod() { return (this.env.CHAT_MOD as DurableObjectNamespace<ChatMod>).get(this.env.CHAT_MOD.idFromName('global')); }

  /** Most restrictive of room mode and global mode; expired modes read as open. */
  private mode(now = Date.now()): ModeState {
    const read = (k: string): ModeState => {
      const raw = this.kv(k);
      if (!raw) return { mode: 'open' };
      const m = JSON.parse(raw) as ModeState;
      return m.until && m.until <= now ? { mode: 'open' } : m;
    };
    const room = read('mode');
    const global = read('global');
    return RANK[global.mode] > RANK[room.mode] ? { ...global, reason: 'global' } : room;
  }
  private broadcast(frame: ServerFrame, except?: WebSocket) {
    const s = encode(frame);
    for (const ws of this.ctx.getWebSockets()) if (ws !== except) { try { ws.send(s); } catch { /* socket gone */ } }
  }
  private changeMode(m: ModeState, key: 'mode' | 'global' = 'mode') {
    this.setKv(key, JSON.stringify(m));
    const eff = this.mode();
    this.broadcast({ t: 'mode', mode: eff.mode, ...(eff.reason ? { reason: eff.reason } : {}), ...(eff.until ? { until: eff.until } : {}) });
  }
  private toMessage(r: Record<string, unknown>, viewerUid?: string): ChatMessage {
    const m: ChatMessage = { id: Number(r.id), ts: Number(r.ts), handle: String(r.handle), body: String(r.body), state: r.state as ChatMessage['state'] };
    if (r.lang) m.lang = String(r.lang);
    if (r.hold_code) m.holdCode = r.hold_code as HoldCode;
    if (r.reason) m.reason = String(r.reason);
    if (viewerUid && r.uid === viewerUid) m.mine = true;
    if (Number(r.moderator) === 1) m.moderator = true;   // rendered as the text "Watchboard (moderation)", never a colour
    return m;
  }
  private history(viewerUid?: string): ChatMessage[] {
    const rows = this.sql(
      `SELECT * FROM messages WHERE state = 'visible' OR (state IN ('held','removed') AND uid = ?)
       ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?`, viewerUid ?? '\u0000', HISTORY_LIMIT);
    return rows.reverse().map((r) => this.toMessage(r, viewerUid));
  }

  async fetch(request: Request): Promise<Response> {
    if (request.headers.get('Upgrade')?.toLowerCase() !== 'websocket') return new Response('upgrade required', { status: 426 });
    if (this.ctx.getWebSockets().length >= LIMITS.maxSockets) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'room_full' }), { status: 503 });
    const now = Date.now();
    // Connections are the cost that readonly cannot reduce, so the connection budget refuses new sockets
    // (the client shows "paused: daily limit"); the message budget makes the room readonly (below).
    if (this.count('room:conns', now) >= LIMITS.roomConnsPerDay) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'daily_budget' }), { status: 503 });
    this.bump('room:conns', now);
    const pair = new WebSocketPair();
    const [client, server] = [pair[0], pair[1]];
    const h = request.headers;
    const conn: Conn = {
      uid: h.get('X-Chat-Uid') ?? undefined, handle: h.get('X-Chat-Handle') ?? undefined,
      uidHmac: h.get('X-Chat-Uid-Hmac') ?? undefined, moderator: h.get('X-Chat-Moderator') === '1', rejects: 0,
    };
    if (!this.kv('slug')) this.setKv('slug', h.get('X-Chat-Room') ?? '');
    this.ctx.acceptWebSocket(server);
    server.serializeAttachment(conn);
    const m = this.mode(now);
    const hello: ServerFrame = {
      t: 'hello', mode: m.mode, history: this.history(conn.uid),
      ...(m.reason ? { reason: m.reason } : {}), ...(m.until ? { until: m.until } : {}),
      ...(conn.uid && conn.handle ? { me: { handle: conn.handle, canWrite: RANK[m.mode] < 2, cooldownMs: 0 } } : {}),
    };
    server.send(encode(hello));
    return new Response(null, { status: 101, webSocket: client });
  }

- [ ] **13.6 Room — frames and RPC** (`worker/chat/room.ts`, second half, inside the class):

```ts
  async webSocketMessage(ws: WebSocket, raw: string | ArrayBuffer) {
    const size = typeof raw === 'string' ? new TextEncoder().encode(raw).length : raw.byteLength;
    if (size > MAX_FRAME_BYTES) { ws.close(1009, 'frame too large'); return; }
    const conn = ws.deserializeAttachment() as Conn;
    const frame = parseClientFrame(raw);
    if (!frame) return this.reject(ws, conn, { t: 'err', code: 'bad_frame' });
    if (frame.t === 'send') return this.onSend(ws, conn, frame);
    if (frame.t === 'delete') return this.onDelete(ws, conn, frame.id);
    return this.onReport(ws, conn, frame.id, frame.category);           // implemented in Task 14.4
  }
  async webSocketClose(ws: WebSocket, code: number) { try { ws.close(code === 1005 ? 1000 : code, 'bye'); } catch { /* already closed */ } }

  private reject(ws: WebSocket, conn: Conn, err: Extract<ServerFrame, { t: 'err' }>) {
    conn.rejects += 1;
    ws.serializeAttachment(conn);
    ws.send(encode(err));
    if (conn.rejects >= LIMITS.maxConsecutiveRejects) ws.close(1008, 'too many rejected frames');
  }
  private accepted(ws: WebSocket, conn: Conn) { if (conn.rejects) { conn.rejects = 0; ws.serializeAttachment(conn); } }
  private alert(code: AlertCode, extra: Omit<AlertContext, 'room'> = {}) {
    const room = this.kv('slug') ?? 'unknown';
    this.ctx.waitUntil(sendAlert(this.env, code, { room, ...extra }).then((ok) => { if (!ok) this.bump('alert:undelivered'); }));
  }
  private async loadTerms(now: number): Promise<TermList | null> {
    if (!this.terms || now - this.terms.at > 5 * 60_000) this.terms = { list: await this.mod().getTerms(), at: now };
    return this.terms.list;
  }

  private async onSend(ws: WebSocket, conn: Conn, f: Extract<ClientFrame, { t: 'send' }>) {
    if (!conn.uid || !conn.handle || !conn.uidHmac) return this.reject(ws, conn, { t: 'err', code: 'auth_required', nonce: f.nonce });
    const prior = this.sql('SELECT id, uid, state, hold_code FROM messages WHERE nonce = ?', f.nonce)[0];
    if (prior) {
      if (prior.uid !== conn.uid) return this.reject(ws, conn, { t: 'err', code: 'bad_frame', nonce: f.nonce });
      const st = prior.state === 'held' ? 'held' : 'visible';
      ws.send(encode({ t: 'ack', nonce: f.nonce, id: Number(prior.id), state: st, ...(prior.hold_code ? { holdCode: prior.hold_code as HoldCode } : {}) }));
      return;
    }
    const now = Date.now();
    const m = this.mode(now);
    if (m.mode === 'closed' || m.mode === 'readonly') return this.reject(ws, conn, { t: 'err', code: m.mode, nonce: f.nonce });
    if (this.sql('SELECT 1 FROM bans WHERE uid_hmac = ? AND until > ?', conn.uidHmac, now).length)
      return this.reject(ws, conn, { t: 'err', code: 'banned', nonce: f.nonce });
    const hourKey = `u:${conn.uid}:h:${utcHour(now)}`;
    const rate = checkIdentity({ lastSendAt: this.count(`u:${conn.uid}:last`, now), hourCount: this.count(hourKey, now), dayCount: this.count(`u:${conn.uid}:d`, now) }, now, m.mode);
    if (!rate.ok) return this.reject(ws, conn, { t: 'err', code: 'rate_limited', nonce: f.nonce, cooldownMs: rate.cooldownMs });

    const body = normalize(f.body);
    const one = (q: string, ...b: unknown[]) => Number(this.sql(q, ...b)[0].c);
    let verdict;
    try {
      verdict = evaluate({
        body, terms: await this.loadTerms(now),
        ownRecent: this.sql('SELECT body FROM messages WHERE uid = ? AND ts > ?', conn.uid, now - 600_000).map((r) => String(r.body)),
        othersSameBody: one('SELECT count(DISTINCT uid) AS c FROM messages WHERE body = ? AND uid != ? AND ts > ?', body, conn.uid, now - 300_000),
        acceptedCount: one("SELECT count(*) AS c FROM messages WHERE uid = ? AND state = 'visible'", conn.uid),
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!(e instanceof FiltersUnavailable)) throw e;
      this.changeMode({ mode: 'readonly', reason: 'filters_unavailable' });
      this.alert('filters_unavailable');
      return this.reject(ws, conn, { t: 'err', code: 'readonly', nonce: f.nonce });
    }
    if (verdict.kind === 'reject') return this.reject(ws, conn, { t: 'err', code: verdict.code, nonce: f.nonce });

    const state = verdict.kind === 'hold' ? 'held' : 'visible';
    const holdCode = verdict.kind === 'hold' ? verdict.code : null;
    this.sql('INSERT INTO messages (ts, uid, handle, body, lang, moderator, state, hold_code, nonce) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',
      now, conn.uid, conn.handle, body, f.lang ?? null, conn.moderator ? 1 : 0, state, holdCode, f.nonce);
    const id = Number(this.sql('SELECT last_insert_rowid() AS id')[0].id);
    this.setCounter(`u:${conn.uid}:last`, now, now);
    this.bump(hourKey, now);
    this.bump(`u:${conn.uid}:d`, now);
    this.accepted(ws, conn);
    // The SQL write above is synchronous; the DO output gate holds this send until it is durable (spec §3.4).
    ws.send(encode({ t: 'ack', nonce: f.nonce, id, state, ...(holdCode ? { holdCode } : {}) }));
    if (state === 'visible') this.broadcast({ t: 'msg', m: this.toMessage(this.sql('SELECT * FROM messages WHERE id = ?', id)[0]) }, ws);
    else await this.mod().recordDecision({ room: this.kv('slug') ?? '', msgId: id, action: 'hold', ground: `filter:${holdCode}`, automated: true });

    const minute = roomMinute(JSON.parse(this.kv('minute') ?? '{"start":0,"count":0}') as { start: number; count: number }, now);
    this.setKv('minute', JSON.stringify({ start: minute.start, count: minute.count }));
    if (minute.triggerSlow && this.mode(now).mode === 'open') {
      this.changeMode({ mode: 'slow', reason: 'auto_slow', until: now + LIMITS.autoSlowMs });
      this.alert('auto_slow');
    }
    const budget = roomBudget(this.bump('room:msgs', now), this.count('room:conns', now));
    if (budget.exhausted) this.changeMode({ mode: 'readonly', reason: 'daily_budget', until: now + msUntilUtcMidnight(now) });
    else if (budget.alert && this.count('alert:budget', now) === 0) { this.bump('alert:budget', now); this.alert('budget_70'); }
  }

  private onDelete(ws: WebSocket, conn: Conn, id: number) {
    const row = this.sql("SELECT uid FROM messages WHERE id = ? AND state IN ('visible','held')", id)[0];
    if (!row || !conn.uid || row.uid !== conn.uid) return this.reject(ws, conn, { t: 'err', code: 'not_author' });
    this.sql("UPDATE messages SET body = '', state = 'deleted' WHERE id = ?", id);
    this.accepted(ws, conn);
    this.broadcast({ t: 'remove', id });
  }

  // Until Task 14.4: reports are refused visibly, never swallowed.
  private onReport(ws: WebSocket, conn: Conn, _id: number, _category: string) {
    return this.reject(ws, conn, { t: 'err', code: 'unknown' });
  }

  // ── RPC used by ChatMod and /admin (Tasks 14-17) ──
  applyBan(uidHmac: string, until: number) {
    this.sql('INSERT OR REPLACE INTO bans (uid_hmac, until) VALUES (?, ?)', uidHmac, until);
    for (const ws of this.ctx.getWebSockets()) {
      const c = ws.deserializeAttachment() as Conn;
      if (c.uidHmac === uidHmac) { ws.send(encode({ t: 'err', code: 'banned' })); ws.close(1008, 'banned'); }
    }
  }
  setMode(mode: ChatMode, reason: ChatModeReason, until?: number) { this.changeMode({ mode, reason, ...(until ? { until } : {}) }); }
  setGlobal(mode: ChatMode, reason?: ChatModeReason, until?: number) { this.changeMode({ mode, ...(reason ? { reason } : {}), ...(until ? { until } : {}) }, 'global'); }
  moderate(msgId: number, action: 'publish' | 'remove', reason: string): boolean {
    const row = this.sql("SELECT * FROM messages WHERE id = ? AND state IN ('visible','held')", msgId)[0];
    if (!row) return false;
    if (action === 'publish') {
      this.sql("UPDATE messages SET state = 'visible', hold_code = NULL WHERE id = ?", msgId);
      this.broadcast({ t: 'msg', m: this.toMessage(this.sql('SELECT * FROM messages WHERE id = ?', msgId)[0]) });
    } else {
      this.sql("UPDATE messages SET body = '', state = 'removed', reason = ? WHERE id = ?", reason, msgId);
      this.broadcast({ t: 'remove', id: msgId, reason });
    }
    return true;
  }
  hideForReports(msgId: number): boolean {
    const changed = this.sql("UPDATE messages SET state = 'held', hold_code = 'reports' WHERE id = ? AND state = 'visible' RETURNING id", msgId).length;
    if (changed) this.broadcast({ t: 'remove', id: msgId });
    return changed > 0;
  }
  eraseUid(uid: string): number {
    const ids = this.sql("SELECT id, state FROM messages WHERE uid = ? AND (body != '' OR state IN ('visible','held'))", uid);
    this.sql("UPDATE messages SET body = '', state = 'deleted', handle = '' WHERE uid = ?", uid);
    const left = Number(this.sql("SELECT count(*) AS c FROM messages WHERE uid = ? AND body != ''", uid)[0].c);
    if (left !== 0) throw new Error(`erasure left ${left} messages`);   // never report a count we did not achieve
    for (const r of ids) if (r.state === 'visible') this.broadcast({ t: 'remove', id: Number(r.id) });
    return ids.length;
  }
  exportUid(uid: string): ChatMessage[] {
    return this.sql("SELECT * FROM messages WHERE uid = ? AND body != '' ORDER BY id", uid).map((r) => this.toMessage(r, uid));
  }
  stats() {
    const now = Date.now();
    return { day: utcDay(now), msgs: this.count('room:msgs', now), conns: this.count('room:conns', now),
      sockets: this.ctx.getWebSockets().length, held: Number(this.sql("SELECT count(*) AS c FROM messages WHERE state = 'held'")[0].c),
      undeliveredAlerts: this.count('alert:undelivered', now), mode: this.mode(now) };
  }
  context(msgId: number, around = 5): ChatMessage[] {
    return this.sql('SELECT * FROM messages WHERE id BETWEEN ? AND ? ORDER BY id', msgId - around, msgId + around).map((r) => this.toMessage(r));
  }
}
```

(`eraseUid` also blanks `handle`, because the handle is personal data too; the row stays only as an id for decisions that reference it.)

- [ ] **13.7 Alerts module** (needed by the room; Task 15 adds the triggers that live outside the room). Create `worker/chat/alerts.ts` and `worker/chat/alerts.test.ts`:

```ts
// alerts.ts — content-free by construction: the context type has no field that could carry message text.
import type { ChatEnv } from './env';
export type AlertCode = 'report_grave' | 'queue_stale' | 'auto_slow' | 'budget_70' | 'filters_unavailable' | 'global_mode' | 'unreviewed_readonly';
export interface AlertContext { room?: string; category?: 'illegal' | 'personal_data'; count?: number; mode?: string }
const TEXT: Record<AlertCode, (c: AlertContext) => string> = {
  report_grave: (c) => `Chat: new ${c.category} report in ${c.room}`,
  queue_stale: (c) => `Chat: ${c.count ?? 1} item(s) waiting > 2 h`,
  auto_slow: (c) => `Chat: ${c.room} switched to slow mode automatically (40 msg/min)`,
  budget_70: (c) => `Chat: ${c.room} passed 70 % of today's budget`,
  filters_unavailable: (c) => `Chat: filters unavailable, ${c.room} is read-only`,
  global_mode: (c) => `Chat: global mode is now ${c.mode}`,
  unreviewed_readonly: (c) => `Chat: ${c.room} went read-only — a grave report waited > 60 min`,
};
export const ADMIN_URL = 'https://chat.watchboard.dev/admin';
export function alertText(code: AlertCode, c: AlertContext): string { return `${TEXT[code](c)} — ${ADMIN_URL}`; }
export async function sendAlert(env: ChatEnv, code: AlertCode, c: AlertContext): Promise<boolean> {
  if (!env.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN || !env.TELEGRAM_ALERT_CHAT_ID) { console.error(`chat alert undelivered (no ops chat configured): ${code}`); return false; }
  const body = new URLSearchParams({ chat_id: env.TELEGRAM_ALERT_CHAT_ID, text: alertText(code, c) });
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`https://api.telegram.org/bot${env.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN}/sendMessage`, { method: 'POST', body });
    return res.ok;
  } catch { return false; }
}
```

```ts
// alerts.test.ts
import { describe, it, expect, vi, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { alertText, sendAlert } from './alerts';

afterEach(() => vi.restoreAllMocks());
describe('alerts', () => {
  it('every alert links to /admin', () => expect(alertText('auto_slow', { room: 'cdmx' })).toContain('https://chat.watchboard.dev/admin'));
  it('posts to the ops chat id, never another', async () => {
    const spy = vi.spyOn(globalThis, 'fetch').mockResolvedValue(new Response('{"ok":true}'));
    expect(await sendAlert({ TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN: 't', TELEGRAM_ALERT_CHAT_ID: '-100ops' } as never, 'budget_70', { room: 'cdmx' })).toBe(true);
    const sent = spy.mock.calls[0][1]?.body as URLSearchParams;
    expect(sent.get('chat_id')).toBe('-100ops');
  });
  it('reports undelivered instead of pretending', async () => {
    expect(await sendAlert({} as never, 'budget_70', { room: 'cdmx' })).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

import { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, resolve } from 'node:path';
import { it, expect } from 'vitest';

const W = resolve(__dirname, '../../worker');
const files = (d: string): string[] => readdirSync(d).flatMap((e) =>
  e === 'node_modules' ? [] : statSync(join(d, e)).isDirectory() ? files(join(d, e)) : e.endsWith('.ts') ? [join(d, e)] : []);
it('worker code never addresses the public Telegram channel', () => {
  const all = files(W);
  expect(all.length).toBeGreaterThan(10);
  for (const f of all) expect(readFileSync(f, 'utf8'), f).not.toContain('TELEGRAM_CHANNEL_ID');
  expect(readFileSync(join(W, 'chat/alerts.ts'), 'utf8')).toContain('TELEGRAM_ALERT_CHAT_ID');
});

```ts
// protocol.ts — new server frame so a report is never silently swallowed
| { t: 'reported'; id: number; ref: number }          // ref = ChatMod report id, shown to the reporter as a reference number

// mod.ts (RPC)
report(r: { room: string; msgId: number; category: ReportCategory; reporterUidHmac?: string; notice?: NoticeJson }): { id: number; hide: boolean };
decide(d: { reportId?: number; room: string; msgId: number; action: 'publish' | 'remove' | 'dismiss'; ground: string; ban?: { uidHmac: string; days: 1 | 7 | 365 } }): { decisionId: number };
ban(uidHmac: string, days: number, ground: string, room: string, msgId: number | null): number;   // returns until
setGlobalMode(mode: ChatMode, reason?: ChatModeReason, until?: number): void;
setRoomMode(slug: string, mode: ChatMode, reason: ChatModeReason, until?: number): void;
erase(uid: string, uidHmac: string): { erased: number; perRoom: Record<string, number> };
exportFor(uid: string): Record<string, ChatMessage[]>;
openReports(): Array<{ id: number; room: string; msgId: number; ts: number; category: string; reporters: number }>;
health(): { globalMode: ModeState; termsVersion: number; termsCount: number; oldestOpenReportMs: number | null; rooms: Record<string, ReturnType<ChatRoom['stats']>> };

// room.ts (RPC, new)
termsChanged(): void;                     // drop the cached list; reopen if the room was readonly for filters_unavailable
heldList(): Array<ChatMessage & { uid: string }>;   // for the admin queue only
authorOf(msgId: number): string | null;   // uid, for bans from the queue
```

import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { connect, cookieFor, mod, room, nonce, TERMS, E } from './helpers';
import { uidHmac } from '../chat/session';
import { resetGlobalCacheForTests } from '../chat/router';

const h = (uid: string) => uidHmac(E.CHAT_BAN_KEY!, uid);
async function post(uid: string, handle: string, body: string, slug = 'test-room') {
  const c = await connect(slug, await cookieFor(uid, handle));
  await c.next('hello');
  c.send({ t: 'send', body, nonce: nonce() });
  return { c, id: (await c.next('ack')).id };
}

describe('ChatMod', () => {
  beforeEach(async () => { await mod().setTerms(TERMS); resetGlobalCacheForTests(); });

  it('three distinct reporters hide a message; the reporter gets a reference', async () => {
    const { id } = await post('gh:201', 'gh/author', 'a claim people dislike');
    const watcher = await connect(); await watcher.next('hello');
    for (const n of [1, 2, 3]) {
      const r = await connect('test-room', await cookieFor(`gh:21${n}`, `gh/r${n}`));
      await r.next('hello');
      r.send({ t: 'report', id, category: 'harassment' });
      expect(await r.next('reported')).toMatchObject({ id });
    }
    expect(await watcher.next('remove')).toMatchObject({ id });
    const st = await runInDurableObject(room(), (_i, s) => s.storage.sql.exec('SELECT state, hold_code FROM messages WHERE id = ?', id).one());
    expect(st).toEqual({ state: 'held', hold_code: 'reports' });
  });

  it('reports from someone whose reports were dismissed 3 times do not count toward hiding', async () => {
    const { id } = await post('gh:202', 'gh/author2', 'another claim');
    const noisy = await h('gh:220');
    await runInDurableObject(mod(), (_i, s) => { s.storage.sql.exec('INSERT OR REPLACE INTO users (uid_hmac, gen, dismissed_reports) VALUES (?, 0, 3)', noisy); });
    expect((await mod().report({ room: 'test-room', msgId: id, category: 'spam', reporterUidHmac: noisy })).hide).toBe(false);
    expect((await mod().report({ room: 'test-room', msgId: id, category: 'spam', reporterUidHmac: await h('gh:221') })).hide).toBe(false);
    expect((await mod().report({ room: 'test-room', msgId: id, category: 'spam', reporterUidHmac: await h('gh:222') })).hide).toBe(false);
  });

  it('remove writes a statement of reasons and the author sees the reason', async () => {
    const { id } = await post('gh:203', 'gh/author3', 'rule-breaking text');
    const { decisionId } = await mod().decide({ room: 'test-room', msgId: id, action: 'remove', ground: 'rules §3 personal data of third parties' });
    const d = await runInDurableObject(mod(), (_i, s) => s.storage.sql.exec('SELECT action, ground, automated FROM decisions WHERE id = ?', decisionId).one());
    expect(d).toEqual({ action: 'remove', ground: 'rules §3 personal data of third parties', automated: 0 });
    const again = await connect('test-room', await cookieFor('gh:203', 'gh/author3'));
    expect((await again.next('hello')).history.find((m) => m.id === id)).toMatchObject({ state: 'removed', reason: 'rules §3 personal data of third parties', body: '' });
  });

  it('a ban closes the socket and revokes the session everywhere', async () => {
    const cookie = await cookieFor('gh:204', 'gh/banned');
    const { c, id } = await post('gh:204', 'gh/banned', 'offending');
    await mod().decide({ room: 'test-room', msgId: id, action: 'remove', ground: 'rules §1', ban: { uidHmac: await h('gh:204'), days: 7 } });
    expect(await c.next('err')).toMatchObject({ code: 'banned' });
    const back = await connect('test-room', cookie);
    expect((await back.next('hello')).me).toBeUndefined();     // gen bumped: old cookie no longer identifies
  });

  it('global closed reaches open sockets and refuses new upgrades', async () => {
    const c = await connect(); await c.next('hello');
    await mod().setGlobalMode('closed', 'owner');
    expect(await c.next('mode')).toMatchObject({ mode: 'closed', reason: 'global' });
    resetGlobalCacheForTests();
    const res = await (await import('cloudflare:workers')).exports.default.fetch('https://chat.watchboard.dev/room/test-room',
      { headers: { Upgrade: 'websocket', Origin: 'https://watchboard.dev' } });
    expect(res.status).toBe(503);
    await mod().setGlobalMode('open');
  });

  it('new terms reopen a room that failed closed', async () => {
    await room('_canary').setMode('readonly', 'filters_unavailable');
    await mod().setTerms(TERMS);
    expect((await room('_canary').stats()).mode.mode).toBe('open');
  });

  it('erasure empties every room and reports the re-read count', async () => {
    await post('gh:205', 'gh/leaver', 'in test-room');
    await post('gh:205', 'gh/leaver', 'in canary', '_canary');
    const res = await mod().erase('gh:205', await h('gh:205'));
    expect(res.erased).toBe(2);

      expect(left).toEqual({ c: 0 });
    }
  });
});

- [ ] **14.3 Implement ChatMod** (add to the class from 13.3; import `parseRooms`, `roomName`, `RETENTION`, `sendAlert`, `ChatRoom`, `ReportCategory`, `ChatMessage`):

```ts
  private rooms(): string[] { return [...parseRooms(this.env.CHAT_ROOMS)]; }
  private room(slug: string) { return (this.env.CHAT_ROOM as DurableObjectNamespace<ChatRoom>).get(this.env.CHAT_ROOM.idFromName(roomName(slug))); }
  private exec(q: string, ...b: unknown[]) { return this.ctx.storage.sql.exec(q, ...b).toArray(); }

  async report(r: { room: string; msgId: number; category: ReportCategory; reporterUidHmac?: string; notice?: unknown }) {
    const now = Date.now();
    this.exec('INSERT INTO reports (room, msg_id, ts, category, reporter_uid_hmac, notice_json) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',
      r.room, r.msgId, now, r.category, r.reporterUidHmac ?? null, r.notice ? JSON.stringify(r.notice) : null);
    const id = Number(this.exec('SELECT last_insert_rowid() AS id')[0].id);
    const grave = r.category === 'illegal' || r.category === 'personal_data';
    const firstGrave = grave && Number(this.exec(
      "SELECT count(*) AS c FROM reports WHERE room = ? AND msg_id = ? AND category IN ('illegal','personal_data')", r.room, r.msgId)[0].c) === 1;
    if (firstGrave) this.ctx.waitUntil(sendAlert(this.env, 'report_grave', { room: r.room, category: r.category as 'illegal' | 'personal_data' }));
    // Only identified reporters with a clean record count toward provisional hiding (spec §4.3).
    const trusted = Number(this.exec(
      `SELECT count(DISTINCT r.reporter_uid_hmac) AS c FROM reports r LEFT JOIN users u ON u.uid_hmac = r.reporter_uid_hmac
       WHERE r.room = ? AND r.msg_id = ? AND r.status = 'open' AND r.reporter_uid_hmac IS NOT NULL
         AND COALESCE(u.dismissed_reports, 0) < 3`, r.room, r.msgId)[0].c);
    return { id, hide: trusted >= 3 };
  }

  async decide(d: { reportId?: number; room: string; msgId: number; action: 'publish' | 'remove' | 'dismiss'; ground: string; ban?: { uidHmac: string; days: 1 | 7 | 365 } }) {
    const now = Date.now();
    if (d.action !== 'dismiss') {
      const ok = await this.room(d.room).moderate(d.msgId, d.action, d.ground);
      if (!ok) throw new Error(`message ${d.room}#${d.msgId} not found or already final`);
    } else {
      // Insert missing reporter rows first, then count the dismissal for every reporter of this message.
      this.exec(`INSERT OR IGNORE INTO users (uid_hmac) SELECT DISTINCT reporter_uid_hmac FROM reports
        WHERE room = ? AND msg_id = ? AND status = 'open' AND reporter_uid_hmac IS NOT NULL`, d.room, d.msgId);
      this.exec(`UPDATE users SET dismissed_reports = dismissed_reports + 1 WHERE uid_hmac IN
        (SELECT reporter_uid_hmac FROM reports WHERE room = ? AND msg_id = ? AND status = 'open')`, d.room, d.msgId);
    }
    const decisionId = this.recordDecision({ room: d.room, msgId: d.msgId, action: d.action, ground: d.ground, automated: false });
    this.exec("UPDATE reports SET status = ? WHERE room = ? AND msg_id = ? AND status = 'open'", d.action === 'dismiss' ? 'dismissed' : 'resolved', d.room, d.msgId);
    if (d.ban) await this.ban(d.ban.uidHmac, d.ban.days, d.ground, d.room, d.msgId, decisionId);
    return { decisionId };
  }

  async ban(uidHmac: string, days: number, ground: string, room: string, msgId: number | null, decisionId?: number) {
    const until = Date.now() + Math.min(days * 86_400_000, RETENTION.maxBanMs);
    this.exec('INSERT OR REPLACE INTO bans (uid_hmac, until, ground, decision_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)', uidHmac, until, ground,
      decisionId ?? this.recordDecision({ room, msgId, action: 'ban', ground, automated: false }));
    this.bumpGen(uidHmac);
    await Promise.all(this.rooms().map((s) => this.room(s).applyBan(uidHmac, until)));
    return until;
  }

  async setGlobalMode(mode: ChatMode, reason?: ChatModeReason, until?: number) {
    this.put('global_mode', JSON.stringify({ mode, ...(reason ? { reason } : {}), ...(until ? { until } : {}) }));
    await Promise.all(this.rooms().map((s) => this.room(s).setGlobal(mode, reason, until)));
    this.ctx.waitUntil(sendAlert(this.env, 'global_mode', { mode }));
  }
  async setRoomMode(slug: string, mode: ChatMode, reason: ChatModeReason, until?: number) {
    if (!this.rooms().includes(slug)) throw new Error(`room ${slug} not in CHAT_ROOMS`);
    await this.room(slug).setMode(mode, reason, until);
  }

  async erase(uid: string, uidHmac: string) {
    const perRoom: Record<string, number> = {};
    for (const s of this.rooms()) perRoom[s] = await this.room(s).eraseUid(uid);
    this.bumpGen(uidHmac);
    return { erased: Object.values(perRoom).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0), perRoom };
  }
  async exportFor(uid: string) {
    const out: Record<string, ChatMessage[]> = {};
    for (const s of this.rooms()) out[s] = await this.room(s).exportUid(uid);
    return out;
  }
  openReports() {
    return this.exec(`SELECT min(id) AS id, room, msg_id AS msgId, min(ts) AS ts, group_concat(DISTINCT category) AS category,
      count(*) AS reporters FROM reports WHERE status = 'open' GROUP BY room, msg_id ORDER BY ts`) as never;
  }
  async health() {
    const t = this.getTerms();
    const oldest = this.exec("SELECT min(ts) AS ts FROM reports WHERE status = 'open'")[0].ts as number | null;
    const rooms: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    for (const s of this.rooms()) rooms[s] = await this.room(s).stats();
    return { globalMode: this.getGlobalMode(), termsVersion: t?.version ?? 0,
      termsCount: t ? Object.values(t.terms).reduce((n, l) => n + l.length, 0) : 0,
      oldestOpenReportMs: oldest === null ? null : Date.now() - oldest, rooms };
  }
```

- [ ] **14.4 Room additions.** In `room.ts`, replace the Task 13 `onReport` with:

```ts
  private async onReport(ws: WebSocket, conn: Conn, id: number, category: ReportCategory) {
    if (!conn.uid || !conn.uidHmac) return this.reject(ws, conn, { t: 'err', code: 'auth_required' });
    const now = Date.now();
    const key = `u:${conn.uid}:r:${utcHour(now)}`;
    if (!checkReport(this.count(key, now))) { this.bump('reports:ignored', now); return this.reject(ws, conn, { t: 'err', code: 'report_limited' }); }
    if (!this.sql("SELECT 1 FROM messages WHERE id = ? AND state = 'visible'", id).length) return this.reject(ws, conn, { t: 'err', code: 'unknown' });
    this.bump(key, now);
    const res = await this.mod().report({ room: this.kv('slug') ?? '', msgId: id, category, reporterUidHmac: conn.uidHmac });
    if (res.hide) this.hideForReports(id);
    this.accepted(ws, conn);
    ws.send(encode({ t: 'reported', id, ref: res.id }));
  }
  termsChanged() {
    this.terms = null;
    const raw = this.kv('mode');
    if (raw && (JSON.parse(raw) as ModeState).reason === 'filters_unavailable') this.changeMode({ mode: 'open' });
  }
  heldList() { return this.sql("SELECT * FROM messages WHERE state = 'held' ORDER BY id").map((r) => ({ ...this.toMessage(r), uid: String(r.uid) })); }
  authorOf(msgId: number): string | null { const r = this.sql('SELECT uid FROM messages WHERE id = ?', msgId)[0]; return r ? String(r.uid) : null; }
```

- [ ] **15.2 Room alarm.** In `room.ts` add, and call `this.ensureAlarm()` at the end of `onSend` (after the insert), and `this.ensureAlarm(m.until)` inside `changeMode` when the new mode has `until`:

```ts
  private ensureAlarm(at?: number) {
    const want = Math.max(Date.now() + 1_000, at ?? Date.now() + 3_600_000);
    this.ctx.waitUntil(this.ctx.storage.getAlarm().then((cur) => {
      if (cur === null || want < cur) return this.ctx.storage.setAlarm(want);
    }));
  }
  async alarm() {
    const now = Date.now();
    this.sql("DELETE FROM messages WHERE ts < ? AND state IN ('visible','removed','deleted')", now - RETENTION.visibleMs);
    this.sql("DELETE FROM messages WHERE ts < ? AND state = 'held'", now - RETENTION.heldMs);
    this.sql('DELETE FROM counters WHERE day < ?', utcDay(now - 2 * 86_400_000));
    this.sql('DELETE FROM bans WHERE until < ?', now);
    for (const key of ['mode', 'global'] as const) {
      const raw = this.kv(key);
      if (raw && (JSON.parse(raw) as ModeState).until && (JSON.parse(raw) as ModeState).until! <= now) this.changeMode({ mode: 'open' }, key);
    }
    const slug = this.kv('slug') ?? '';
    const since = await this.mod().graveOpenSince(slug);
    const cur = this.mode(now);
    if (since !== null && now - since > 60 * 60_000 && cur.mode !== 'readonly' && cur.mode !== 'closed') {
      this.changeMode({ mode: 'readonly', reason: 'unreviewed_report' });
      this.alert('unreviewed_readonly');
    }
    const anything = Number(this.sql('SELECT count(*) AS c FROM messages')[0].c) > 0;
    const until = this.mode(now).until;
    if (anything || since !== null || until) await this.ctx.storage.setAlarm(Math.min(now + 3_600_000, until ?? Infinity));
  }
  clearUnreviewed() {
    const raw = this.kv('mode');
    if (raw && (JSON.parse(raw) as ModeState).reason === 'unreviewed_report') this.changeMode({ mode: 'open' });
  }
```

(import `RETENTION` from `./limits`). A room with no messages, no open grave report and no timed mode schedules nothing — it sleeps for free.

- [ ] **15.3 Mod alarm.** In `mod.ts` add, and call `this.ensureAlarm()` at the end of `report`, `setGlobalMode`, `ban` and `setTerms`:

```ts
  private ensureAlarm() {
    this.ctx.waitUntil(this.ctx.storage.getAlarm().then((cur) => { if (cur === null) return this.ctx.storage.setAlarm(Date.now() + 3_600_000); }));
  }
  graveOpenSince(room: string): number | null {
    const r = this.exec("SELECT min(ts) AS ts FROM reports WHERE room = ? AND status = 'open' AND category IN ('illegal','personal_data')", room)[0];
    return r?.ts == null ? null : Number(r.ts);
  }
  async alarm() {
    const now = Date.now();
    this.exec('DELETE FROM reports WHERE ts < ?', now - RETENTION.modRecordsMs);
    this.exec('DELETE FROM decisions WHERE ts < ?', now - RETENTION.modRecordsMs);
    this.exec('DELETE FROM bans WHERE until < ?', now);
    const cutoff = now - 2 * 3_600_000;                                  // "waiting > 2 h"
    const lastCutoff = Number(this.get('stale_alerted_upto') ?? 0);      // cutoff used by the previous alert
    const count = (q: string, ...b: unknown[]) => Number(this.exec(q, ...b)[0].c);
    const newlyStale = count("SELECT count(*) AS c FROM reports WHERE status = 'open' AND ts < ? AND ts >= ?", cutoff, lastCutoff);
    if (newlyStale > 0) {
      this.put('stale_alerted_upto', String(cutoff));
      this.ctx.waitUntil(sendAlert(this.env, 'queue_stale', { count: count("SELECT count(*) AS c FROM reports WHERE status = 'open' AND ts < ?", cutoff) }));
    }
    const open = Number(this.exec("SELECT count(*) AS c FROM reports WHERE status = 'open'")[0].c);
    const rows = Number(this.exec('SELECT (SELECT count(*) FROM reports) + (SELECT count(*) FROM decisions) + (SELECT count(*) FROM bans) AS c')[0].c);
    if (open > 0 || rows > 0) await this.ctx.storage.setAlarm(now + 3_600_000);
  }
```

The stale alert repeats at most once per new stale report (a report that was already stale when the last alert went out does not re-alert every hour). In `decide`, after updating `reports`, add: `if (this.graveOpenSince(d.room) === null) await this.room(d.room).clearUnreviewed();`.

import { verifySession } from '../chat/session';
import { E, mod, TERMS } from './helpers';

}

}

  beforeEach(async () => { await mod().setTerms(TERMS); });

export const esc = (s: string) =>
  s.replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' })[c]!);
const CSP = "default-src 'none'; style-src 'unsafe-inline'; form-action 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none'; base-uri 'none'";
/** Worker-served HTML. `bodyHtml` must already be escaped; callers build it with esc(). */
export function page(title: string, bodyHtml: string, status = 200): Response {
  const html = `<!doctype html><html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">
<title>${esc(title)}</title><style>body{font:16px/1.5 system-ui,sans-serif;max-width:40rem;margin:2rem auto;padding:0 16px}
button,input,select,textarea{font:inherit;min-height:44px}</style></head><body>${bodyHtml}</body></html>`;
  return new Response(html, { status, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/html; charset=utf-8', 'Cache-Control': 'no-store',
    'Content-Security-Policy': CSP, 'X-Content-Type-Options': 'nosniff', 'Referrer-Policy': 'no-referrer' } });
}

- [ ] **16.4 OAuth implementation.** `worker/chat/oauth.ts` (below the facts comment from 16.1):

```ts
import { b64url, fromB64url, requireSecret, sign, verifySig } from './crypto';
import { accountOldEnough, isReservedHandle, minAccountAgeDays, readCookie, sessionCookie, signSession, SESSION_TTL_S, uidHmac } from './session';
import { parseOrigins, type ChatEnv } from './env';
import { esc, page } from './pages';
import { modStub } from './router';   // used inside functions only, so the router↔oauth import cycle is safe

export interface OAuthDeps { fetch: typeof fetch; now: () => number; random: (n: number) => Uint8Array }
export const defaultDeps: OAuthDeps = { fetch: (i, init) => fetch(i, init), now: () => Date.now(), random: (n) => crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(n)) };
const OAUTH_COOKIE = '__Host-wb_oauth';
const clearOauth = `${OAUTH_COOKIE}=; Secure; HttpOnly; SameSite=Lax; Path=/; Max-Age=0`;
const siteOrigin = (env: ChatEnv) => [...parseOrigins(env.CHAT_ALLOWED_ORIGINS)][0] ?? 'https://watchboard.dev';

export function safeReturnPath(raw: string | null): string {
  return raw && raw.startsWith('/') && !raw.startsWith('//') && /^\/[a-z0-9\/_-]*$/i.test(raw) ? raw : '/';
}
async function challenge(verifier: string) {
  return b64url(await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', new TextEncoder().encode(verifier)));
}

export async function authStart(req: Request, env: ChatEnv, deps: OAuthDeps = defaultDeps): Promise<Response> {
  const key = requireSecret(env.CHAT_SESSION_KEY, 'CHAT_SESSION_KEY');
  const url = new URL(req.url);
  const state = b64url(deps.random(16));
  const verifier = b64url(deps.random(32));
  const data = b64url(new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify({ state, verifier, ret: safeReturnPath(url.searchParams.get('return')), exp: deps.now() + 600_000 })));
  const gh = new URL('https://github.com/login/oauth/authorize');
  gh.search = new URLSearchParams({ client_id: env.GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID ?? '', redirect_uri: `https://${url.host}/auth/github/callback`,
    scope: 'read:user', state, code_challenge: await challenge(verifier), code_challenge_method: 'S256', allow_signup: 'false' }).toString();
  await modStub(env).countAuth('start');
  return new Response(null, { status: 302, headers: { Location: gh.toString(), 'Cache-Control': 'no-store',
    'Set-Cookie': `${OAUTH_COOKIE}=${data}.${await sign(key, data)}; Secure; HttpOnly; SameSite=Lax; Path=/; Max-Age=600` } });
}

function refuse(status: number, title: string, text: string, env: ChatEnv): Response {
  const res = page(title, `<h1>${esc(title)}</h1><p>${esc(text)}</p><p><a href="${esc(siteOrigin(env))}/">Back to Watchboard</a></p>`, status);
  res.headers.append('Set-Cookie', clearOauth);
  return res;
}

export async function authCallback(req: Request, env: ChatEnv, deps: OAuthDeps = defaultDeps): Promise<Response> {
  const key = requireSecret(env.CHAT_SESSION_KEY, 'CHAT_SESSION_KEY');
  const url = new URL(req.url);
  const raw = readCookie(req, OAUTH_COOKIE);
  const [data, sig] = raw?.split('.') ?? [];
  if (!data || !sig || !(await verifySig(key, data, sig))) return refuse(400, 'Sign-in expired', 'Please start again from the chat panel.', env);
  const st = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(fromB64url(data))) as { state: string; verifier: string; ret: string; exp: number };
  if (st.exp < deps.now() || url.searchParams.get('state') !== st.state || !url.searchParams.get('code'))
    return refuse(400, 'Sign-in expired', 'Please start again from the chat panel.', env);
  const mod = modStub(env);
  try {
    const tok = await deps.fetch('https://github.com/login/oauth/access_token', { method: 'POST',
      headers: { Accept: 'application/json', 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' },
      body: new URLSearchParams({ client_id: env.GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID ?? '', client_secret: env.GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET ?? '',
        code: url.searchParams.get('code')!, redirect_uri: `https://${url.host}/auth/github/callback`, code_verifier: st.verifier }).toString() });
    const { access_token } = (await tok.json()) as { access_token?: string };
    if (!access_token) throw new Error('no access_token');
    const u = await deps.fetch('https://api.github.com/user', { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${access_token}`,
      Accept: 'application/vnd.github+json', 'User-Agent': 'watchboard-chat' } });
    if (!u.ok) throw new Error(`github user ${u.status}`);
    // The GitHub token is dropped here: only id, login and created_at are kept (spec §3.6).
    const { id, login, created_at } = (await u.json()) as { id: number; login: string; created_at: string };
    const minDays = minAccountAgeDays(env);
    if (isReservedHandle(login)) { await mod.countAuth('refused_reserved'); return refuse(403, 'Handle not available', 'This GitHub login is reserved in the Watchboard chat. You can still read.', env); }
    if (!accountOldEnough(created_at, deps.now(), minDays)) {
      await mod.countAuth('refused_age');
      return refuse(403, 'Account too new', `To write in the chat your GitHub account must be at least ${minDays} days old. You can still read.`, env);
    }
    const uid = `gh:${id}`;
    const gen = await mod.getGen(await uidHmac(requireSecret(env.CHAT_BAN_KEY, 'CHAT_BAN_KEY'), uid));
    const iat = Math.floor(deps.now() / 1000);
    const token = await signSession({ uid, handle: `gh/${login}`, iat, exp: iat + SESSION_TTL_S, gen }, key);
    await mod.countAuth('done');
    const res = new Response(null, { status: 302, headers: { Location: `${siteOrigin(env)}${st.ret}#community`, 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' } });
    res.headers.append('Set-Cookie', sessionCookie(token));
    res.headers.append('Set-Cookie', clearOauth);
    return res;
  } catch {
    await mod.countAuth('failed');
    return refuse(502, 'GitHub sign-in failed', 'GitHub did not answer as expected. Please try again later.', env);
  }
}
```

In `mod.ts` add (and put `auth: this.authCounts()` in the object `health()` returns):

```ts
  countAuth(kind: 'start' | 'done' | 'refused_age' | 'refused_reserved' | 'failed') {
    const k = `auth:${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}:${kind}`;
    this.exec("INSERT INTO settings (k, v) VALUES (?, '1') ON CONFLICT(k) DO UPDATE SET v = CAST(v AS INTEGER) + 1", k);
  }
  authCounts(): Record<string, number> {
    const p = `auth:${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}:`;
    return Object.fromEntries(this.exec('SELECT k, v FROM settings WHERE k LIKE ?', `${p}%`).map((r) => [String(r.k).slice(p.length), Number(r.v)]));
  }

  });

- [ ] **16.6 Implement.** `worker/chat/me.ts`:

```ts
import { parseOrigins, type ChatEnv } from './env';
import { chatJson } from './http';
import { clearSessionCookie } from './session';
import { identityFromCookie, modStub } from './router';

function cors(req: Request, env: ChatEnv): Record<string, string> {
  const o = req.headers.get('Origin');
  return o && parseOrigins(env.CHAT_ALLOWED_ORIGINS).has(o)
    ? { 'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': o, 'Access-Control-Allow-Credentials': 'true', Vary: 'Origin' } : {};
}
const originOk = (req: Request, env: ChatEnv) => parseOrigins(env.CHAT_ALLOWED_ORIGINS).has(req.headers.get('Origin') ?? '');
export function preflight(req: Request, env: ChatEnv): Response {
  return new Response(null, { status: 204, headers: { ...cors(req, env), 'Access-Control-Allow-Methods': 'GET, POST', 'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'Content-Type', 'Access-Control-Max-Age': '600' } });
}
export async function logout(req: Request, env: ChatEnv, everywhere: boolean): Promise<Response> {
  if (!originOk(req, env)) return chatJson({ error: 'forbidden_origin' }, 403);
  const id = await identityFromCookie(req, env);
  if (everywhere && id) await modStub(env).bumpGen(id.uidHmac);
  return chatJson({ ok: true, everywhere: everywhere && !!id }, 200, { ...cors(req, env), 'Set-Cookie': clearSessionCookie() });
}
export async function eraseMe(req: Request, env: ChatEnv): Promise<Response> {
  if (!originOk(req, env)) return chatJson({ error: 'forbidden_origin' }, 403);
  const id = await identityFromCookie(req, env);
  if (!id) return chatJson({ error: 'auth_required' }, 401, cors(req, env));
  const res = await modStub(env).erase(id.uid, id.uidHmac);   // counts are re-read inside each room (Task 13.6)
  return chatJson(res, 200, { ...cors(req, env), 'Set-Cookie': clearSessionCookie() });
}
export async function exportMe(req: Request, env: ChatEnv): Promise<Response> {
  const id = await identityFromCookie(req, env);
  if (!id) return chatJson({ error: 'auth_required' }, 401, cors(req, env));
  const rooms = await modStub(env).exportFor(id.uid);
  return chatJson({ exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(), handle: id.handle, rooms }, 200,
    { ...cors(req, env), 'Content-Disposition': 'attachment; filename="watchboard-chat-export.json"' });
}
```

  if (path === '/me/erase' && request.method === 'POST') return eraseMe(request, env);

```ts
// limits.ts — closed list; the statement of reasons (DSA art. 17) is always one of these
export const GROUNDS = ['illegal content', 'rules §1 harassment or hate', 'rules §2 spam or flooding',
  'rules §3 personal data of others', 'rules §4 dangerous misinformation', 'rules §5 impersonation'] as const;
// admin.ts
export async function handleAdmin(req: Request, env: ChatEnv, path: string): Promise<Response>;
// GET  /admin              queue: open reports + held messages of every room, oldest first, 5 messages of context each
// GET  /admin/health       JSON of ChatMod.health()
// POST /admin/decide       room, msgId, action=publish|remove|dismiss, ground ∈ GROUNDS, ban=0|1|7|365
// POST /admin/mode         scope=global|<slug>, mode, minutes (optional; 0 = no expiry)
// GET|POST /admin/terms    one textarea per locale, one term per line; the terms are never logged or alerted
// POST /admin/erase        login → GitHub numeric id (public API) → ChatMod.erase; shows the re-read count
// POST /admin/purge        room, confirm (must equal the slug) → ChatRoom.purge()
// room.ts
purge(): number;   // deletes every message row; returns the count re-read as deleted; broadcasts a 'mode' frame so open panels reload
```

Account age is **not** shown in the queue (spec §4.4 asks for it): it is checked once at login and never stored, which is the smaller data footprint. Recorded as D7 in ADR-0003.

- [ ] **17.2 Implement.** `worker/chat/admin.ts`:

```ts
import type { ChatMode } from '../../src/lib/community/types';
import { requireSecret } from './crypto';
import { parseRooms, type ChatEnv } from './env';
import { chatJson } from './http';
import { GROUNDS, roomName } from './limits';
import { esc, page } from './pages';
import { identityFromCookie, modStub } from './router';
import { uidHmac } from './session';
import type { ChatRoom } from './room';

const MODES: readonly ChatMode[] = ['open', 'slow', 'readonly', 'closed'];
const LOCALES = ['en', 'es', 'fr', 'pt'] as const;
const roomStub = (env: ChatEnv, slug: string) =>
  (env.CHAT_ROOM as DurableObjectNamespace<ChatRoom>).get(env.CHAT_ROOM.idFromName(roomName(slug)));
const back = () => new Response(null, { status: 303, headers: { Location: '/admin', 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' } });
const bad = (msg: string, status = 400) => page('Refused', `<p>${esc(msg)}</p><p><a href="/admin">Back</a></p>`, status);
const opts = (list: readonly (string | number)[]) => list.map((v) => `<option>${esc(String(v))}</option>`).join('');

async function queuePage(env: ChatEnv, rooms: string[]): Promise<Response> {
  const mod = modStub(env);
  const reports = await mod.openReports();
  const parts: string[] = [];
  const item = async (slug: string, msgId: number, label: string) => {
    const ctx = await roomStub(env, slug).context(msgId, 5);
    const lines = ctx.map((m) => `<li${m.id === msgId ? ' style="font-weight:bold"' : ''}>${esc(m.handle)}: ${esc(m.body || `[${m.state}]`)}</li>`).join('');
    parts.push(`<section style="border:1px solid #888;padding:8px;margin:12px 0"><p>${esc(slug)} #${msgId} · ${esc(label)}</p><ol>${lines}</ol>
<form method="post" action="/admin/decide"><input type="hidden" name="room" value="${esc(slug)}"><input type="hidden" name="msgId" value="${msgId}">
<select name="action">${opts(['remove', 'publish', 'dismiss'])}</select> <select name="ground">${opts(GROUNDS)}</select>
<select name="ban">${opts([0, 1, 7, 365])}</select> <button>Apply</button></form></section>`);
  };
  for (const r of reports) await item(r.room, r.msgId, `report: ${r.category} · ${r.reporters} reporter(s) · ${Math.round((Date.now() - r.ts) / 60_000)} min`);
  for (const slug of rooms) for (const m of await roomStub(env, slug).heldList()) {
    if (!reports.some((r) => r.room === slug && r.msgId === m.id)) await item(slug, m.id, `held: ${m.holdCode ?? ''}`);
  }
  const modeForm = `<form method="post" action="/admin/mode"><select name="scope">${opts(['global', ...rooms])}</select>
<select name="mode">${opts(MODES)}</select> minutes <input name="minutes" value="0" inputmode="numeric" size="4"> <button>Set mode</button></form>`;
  return page('Chat admin', `<h1>Queue (${parts.length})</h1>${parts.join('') || '<p>Nothing waiting.</p>'}<h2>Modes</h2>${modeForm}
<p><a href="/admin/terms">Term list</a> · <a href="/admin/health">Health JSON</a></p>
<h2>Erase a user</h2><form method="post" action="/admin/erase">GitHub login <input name="login"> <button>Erase</button></form>
<h2>Purge a room's history</h2><form method="post" action="/admin/purge"><select name="room">${opts(rooms)}</select> type the slug to confirm <input name="confirm"> <button>Purge</button></form>`);
}

export async function handleAdmin(req: Request, env: ChatEnv, path: string): Promise<Response> {
  const id = await identityFromCookie(req, env);
  if (!id?.moderator) return page('Moderators only', '<p>Moderators only.</p><p><a href="/auth/github/start?return=/__admin">Sign in with GitHub</a></p>', 403);
  // Same-origin check on every action (spec §6.4); SameSite=Lax alone would still allow top-level POSTs from elsewhere.
  if (req.method === 'POST' && req.headers.get('Origin') !== new URL(req.url).origin) return bad('Cross-origin action refused.', 403);
  const mod = modStub(env);
  const rooms = [...parseRooms(env.CHAT_ROOMS)];
  const f = req.method === 'POST' ? await req.formData() : null;
  const s = (k: string) => String(f?.get(k) ?? '');
  switch (`${req.method} ${path}`) {
    case 'GET /admin': return queuePage(env, rooms);
    case 'GET /admin/health': return chatJson(await mod.health());
    case 'POST /admin/decide': {
      const slug = s('room'), msgId = Number(s('msgId')), action = s('action'), ground = s('ground'), days = Number(s('ban'));
      if (!rooms.includes(slug) || !Number.isInteger(msgId) || !['publish', 'remove', 'dismiss'].includes(action)
        || !(GROUNDS as readonly string[]).includes(ground) || ![0, 1, 7, 365].includes(days)) return bad('Invalid decision.');
      let ban: { uidHmac: string; days: 1 | 7 | 365 } | undefined;
      if (days > 0) {
        const uid = await roomStub(env, slug).authorOf(msgId);
        if (!uid) return bad('Author not found.');
        ban = { uidHmac: await uidHmac(requireSecret(env.CHAT_BAN_KEY, 'CHAT_BAN_KEY'), uid), days: days as 1 | 7 | 365 };
      }
      await mod.decide({ room: slug, msgId, action: action as 'publish' | 'remove' | 'dismiss', ground, ...(ban ? { ban } : {}) });
      return back();
    }
    case 'POST /admin/mode': {
      const scope = s('scope'), mode = s('mode') as ChatMode, minutes = Number(s('minutes') || '0');
      if (!MODES.includes(mode) || !Number.isFinite(minutes) || minutes < 0) return bad('Invalid mode.');
      const until = minutes > 0 ? Date.now() + minutes * 60_000 : undefined;
      if (scope === 'global') await mod.setGlobalMode(mode, 'owner', until);
      else if (rooms.includes(scope)) await mod.setRoomMode(scope, mode, 'owner', until);
      else return bad('Unknown room.');
      return back();
    }
    case 'GET /admin/terms': {
      const t = await mod.getTerms();
      const areas = LOCALES.map((l) => `<p>${l}<br><textarea name="${l}" rows="8" cols="40">${esc((t?.terms[l] ?? []).join('\n'))}</textarea></p>`).join('');
      return page('Term list', `<h1>Term list v${t?.version ?? 0}</h1><form method="post" action="/admin/terms">${areas}<button>Save new version</button></form>`);
    }
    case 'POST /admin/terms': {
      const terms = Object.fromEntries(LOCALES.map((l) => [l, s(l).split('\n').map((x) => x.trim()).filter(Boolean)])) as Record<(typeof LOCALES)[number], string[]>;
      const total = Object.values(terms).reduce((n, l) => n + l.length, 0);
      if (total === 0) return bad('An empty list would make every room read-only (filters fail closed). Nothing saved.');
      const version = await mod.setTerms(terms);
      return page('Saved', `<p>Term list v${version} saved: ${total} terms.</p><p><a href="/admin">Back</a></p>`);
    }
    case 'POST /admin/erase': {
      const login = s('login').trim();
      if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9-]{1,39}$/.test(login)) return bad('Not a GitHub login.');
      const gh = await fetch(`https://api.github.com/users/${login}`, { headers: { 'User-Agent': 'watchboard-chat', Accept: 'application/vnd.github+json' } });
      if (!gh.ok) return bad(`GitHub answered ${gh.status}; nothing erased.`, 502);
      const uid = `gh:${((await gh.json()) as { id: number }).id}`;
      const r = await mod.erase(uid, await uidHmac(requireSecret(env.CHAT_BAN_KEY, 'CHAT_BAN_KEY'), uid));
      return page('Erased', `<p>Erased ${r.erased} messages of ${esc(login)} (re-read per room: ${esc(JSON.stringify(r.perRoom))}).</p><p><a href="/admin">Back</a></p>`);
    }
    case 'POST /admin/purge': {
      const slug = s('room');
      if (!rooms.includes(slug) || s('confirm') !== slug) return bad('Type the room slug exactly to confirm.');
      const n = await roomStub(env, slug).purge();
      return page('Purged', `<p>Deleted ${n} messages from ${esc(slug)}.</p><p><a href="/admin">Back</a></p>`);
    }
    default: return bad('Not found.', 404);
  }
}
```

In `room.ts` add:

```ts
  purge(): number {
    const before = Number(this.sql('SELECT count(*) AS c FROM messages')[0].c);
    this.sql('DELETE FROM messages');
    const after = Number(this.sql('SELECT count(*) AS c FROM messages')[0].c);
    if (after !== 0) throw new Error(`purge left ${after} rows`);
    const m = this.mode();
    this.broadcast({ t: 'mode', mode: m.mode, ...(m.reason ? { reason: m.reason } : {}), ...(m.until ? { until: m.until } : {}) });
    return before;
  }
```

- [ ] **18.2 Implement.** `worker/chat/notice.ts`:

```ts
import { parseRooms, type ChatEnv } from './env';
import { esc, page } from './pages';
import { modStub } from './router';

export type NoticeKind = 'illegal' | 'csam' | 'terrorism' | 'threat';
export interface NoticeJson { kind: NoticeKind; explanation: string; name?: string; email?: string; goodFaith: true; submittedAt: number }
const KINDS: Record<NoticeKind, string> = {
  illegal: 'Illegal content (other)', csam: 'Child sexual abuse material', terrorism: 'Terrorist content', threat: 'Threat to life or safety',
};
const EMAIL_RE = /^[^\s@<>]{1,64}@[^\s@<>]{1,190}\.[a-z]{2,}$/i;

export function parseNotice(f: FormData, rooms: Set<string>) {
  const s = (k: string) => String(f.get(k) ?? '').trim();
  const room = s('room'), msgId = Number(s('msgId')), kind = s('kind') as NoticeKind, explanation = s('explanation');
  const name = s('name'), email = s('email');
  if (!rooms.has(room) || !Number.isInteger(msgId) || msgId <= 0) return { error: 'The room or message number is not valid.' };
  if (!(kind in KINDS)) return { error: 'Choose a type of notice.' };
  if (explanation.length < 20 || explanation.length > 5000) return { error: 'Explain in 20 to 5000 characters why the content is illegal.' };
  if (email && !EMAIL_RE.test(email)) return { error: 'The email address is not valid.' };
  if (name.length > 200) return { error: 'The name is too long.' };
  if (s('goodFaith') !== 'on') return { error: 'The statement of good faith is required (DSA art. 16(2)(d)).' };
  return { room, msgId, notice: { kind, explanation, goodFaith: true as const, ...(name ? { name } : {}), ...(email ? { email } : {}) } };
}

function form(env: ChatEnv, room: string, id: string, error?: string): Response {
  const contact = env.CHAT_CONTACT_EMAIL
    ? `Contact point (DSA arts. 11-12): <a href="mailto:${esc(env.CHAT_CONTACT_EMAIL)}">${esc(env.CHAT_CONTACT_EMAIL)}</a>`
    : 'Contact point: not yet published.';
  const kinds = Object.entries(KINDS).map(([k, v]) => `<label><input type="radio" name="kind" value="${k}" required> ${esc(v)}</label><br>`).join('');
  return page('Report illegal content', `<h1>Report illegal content</h1>${error ? `<p role="alert"><strong>${esc(error)}</strong></p>` : ''}
<p>Use this form to notify Watchboard of chat content you consider illegal (EU Digital Services Act, art. 16). No account is needed.
For breaches of the chat rules that are not illegal, use "Report" in the chat.</p>
<form method="post" action="/notice">
<p>Room <input name="room" value="${esc(room)}" required> Message number <input name="msgId" value="${esc(id)}" inputmode="numeric" required></p>
<fieldset><legend>Type</legend>${kinds}</fieldset>
<p><label>Why is it illegal? (required)<br><textarea name="explanation" rows="6" cols="40" minlength="20" maxlength="5000" required></textarea></label></p>
<p><label>Your name (optional; not needed for child sexual abuse material)<br><input name="name" maxlength="200"></label></p>
<p><label>Your email (optional; used only to follow up on this notice)<br><input name="email" type="email"></label></p>
<p><label><input type="checkbox" name="goodFaith"> I confirm in good faith that the information and allegations in this notice are accurate and complete.</label></p>
<p><button>Send notice</button></p></form><p>${contact}</p>`, error ? 400 : 200);
}

export async function handleNotice(req: Request, env: ChatEnv): Promise<Response> {
  const url = new URL(req.url);
  if (req.method === 'GET') return form(env, url.searchParams.get('room') ?? '', url.searchParams.get('id') ?? '');
  if (req.method !== 'POST') return page('Not allowed', '<p>Method not allowed.</p>', 405);
  if (req.headers.get('Origin') !== url.origin) return page('Refused', '<p>Cross-origin submission refused.</p>', 403);
  const f = await req.formData();
  const parsed = parseNotice(f, parseRooms(env.CHAT_ROOMS));
  if ('error' in parsed) return form(env, String(f.get('room') ?? ''), String(f.get('msgId') ?? ''), parsed.error);
  const mod = modStub(env);
  if (!(await mod.noticeAllowed())) return page('Try again later', `<p>Too many notices this hour. ${env.CHAT_CONTACT_EMAIL ? `Write to ${esc(env.CHAT_CONTACT_EMAIL)}.` : 'Please try again in an hour.'}</p>`, 429);
  const notice: NoticeJson = { ...parsed.notice, submittedAt: Date.now() };
  const { id } = await mod.report({ room: parsed.room, msgId: parsed.msgId, category: 'illegal', notice });
  // No email provider exists (spec §1): the acknowledgement of receipt is this page, with a reference the notifier can quote.
  return page('Notice received', `<h1>Notice received</h1><p>Reference: ${id}</p><p>It is in the moderation queue. Keep the reference number;
the decision on the content will be shown in the chat in place of the message.</p>`);
}
```

In `mod.ts` add:

```ts
  noticeAllowed(): boolean {
    const k = `notice:${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 13)}`;
    const n = Number(this.get(k) ?? 0);
    if (n >= NOTICES_PER_HOUR) return false;
    this.put(k, String(n + 1));
    return true;
  }
```

- [ ] **19.3 Live test.** `tests/live/chat.live.test.ts` (Node's global `WebSocket` honours a `headers` option — checked on Node 26.7 against a local upgrade server that logged `origin= https://watchboard.dev`; if a runtime ignores it, the upgrade gets 403 and the test fails visibly):

```ts
import { request } from 'node:https';
import { expect } from 'vitest';
import { liveDescribe, liveIt, LIVE_TEST_TIMEOUT_MS } from '../helpers/live';

const HOST = 'chat.watchboard.dev';
liveDescribe('chat.watchboard.dev (canary room)', () => {
  liveIt('health reports a deployed version and a loaded term list', async () => {
    const h = await (await fetch(`https://${HOST}/health`)).json() as { enabled: boolean; version: string; termsCount: number };
    expect(h.enabled).toBe(true);
    expect(h.version).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{40}$/);
    expect(h.termsCount).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  }, LIVE_TEST_TIMEOUT_MS);
  liveIt('the canary room answers hello with a mode', async () => {
    const ws = new WebSocket(`wss://${HOST}/room/_canary`, { headers: { Origin: 'https://watchboard.dev' } } as never);
    const hello = await new Promise<{ t: string; mode: string }>((resolve, reject) => {
      ws.onmessage = (e) => { resolve(JSON.parse(String(e.data))); };
      ws.onerror = () => { reject(new Error('socket error')); };
      setTimeout(() => { reject(new Error('no hello in 15 s')); }, 15_000);
    });
    ws.close();
    expect(hello.t).toBe('hello');
    expect(['open', 'slow', 'readonly', 'closed']).toContain(hello.mode);
  }, LIVE_TEST_TIMEOUT_MS);
  liveIt('a foreign Origin is refused with 403 before the room', async () => {
    const status = await new Promise<number>((resolve, reject) => {
      const req = request({ host: HOST, path: '/room/_canary', headers: { Connection: 'Upgrade', Upgrade: 'websocket', 'Sec-WebSocket-Version': '13',
        'Sec-WebSocket-Key': Buffer.from('0123456789abcdef').toString('base64'), Origin: 'https://evil.example' } });
      req.on('response', (res) => { resolve(res.statusCode ?? 0); res.resume(); });
      req.on('upgrade', () => { reject(new Error('upgraded with a foreign Origin')); });
      req.on('error', reject);
      req.end();
    });
    expect(status).toBe(403);
  }, LIVE_TEST_TIMEOUT_MS);
});
```

import { readFileSync, readdirSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';

import type { TrackerConfig } from '../tracker-config';

```bash
# check_absent PATH FORBIDDEN_SUBSTRING LABEL — the page answers 200 and does NOT contain the substring.
check_absent() {
  local path="$1" forbid="$2" label="$3" code
  code=$(curl -sS -o /tmp/smoke-body -w '%{http_code}' "http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}${path}") || code=000
  if [ "$code" = "200" ] && ! grep -q -- "$forbid" /tmp/smoke-body; then
    echo "| $label ($path) | ✅ 200, no \`$forbid\` |" >> smoke-summary.md
  else
    echo "| $label ($path) | ❌ $code or contains \`$forbid\` |" >> smoke-summary.md; fail=1
  fi
}
```

### Task 23: Client state machine (reconnect, ack timeout, visible states) and the `chat.*` strings

No gate. Spec §3.4, §5.3, §5.4. Branch `feat/chat-panel`. Pure TypeScript, unit-tested in node; no React yet.

**Files:** Create `src/lib/community/chat-client.ts`, `src/lib/community/chat-client.test.ts`. Modify `src/lib/community/protocol.ts` (`ACK_TIMEOUT_MS`), `src/i18n/translations.ts`.

**Interfaces:**

```ts
// protocol.ts
export const ACK_TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000;
// chat-client.ts
export type ConnState = 'idle' | 'connecting' | 'open' | 'retrying' | 'failed';
export interface PendingSend { nonce: string; body: string; lang?: string; status: 'sending' | 'failed'; error?: ChatErrorCode }
export interface ChatState {
  conn: ConnState; loadedOnce: boolean; attempts: number;
  mode: ChatMode; reason?: ChatModeReason; until?: number;
  messages: ChatMessage[]; pending: PendingSend[];
  me?: { handle: string; canWrite: boolean; cooldownMs: number };
  lastError?: ChatErrorCode; lastReported?: { id: number; ref: number };
}
export interface SocketLike {
  send(data: string): void; close(code?: number): void;
  onopen: (() => void) | null; onmessage: ((e: { data: unknown }) => void) | null;
  onclose: ((e: { code: number }) => void) | null; onerror: (() => void) | null;
}
export interface ClientDeps {
  connect: (url: string) => SocketLike; now: () => number; random: () => number;
  setTimer: (fn: () => void, ms: number) => unknown; clearTimer: (id: unknown) => void; nonce: () => string;
}
export const BACKOFF_MS: readonly number[];          // [1_000, 2_000, 5_000, 10_000, 30_000]; after the last one → 'failed'
export const NO_RETRY_CODES: readonly number[];      // [1008] — banned / policy: reconnecting would only repeat it
export const initialState: ChatState;
export function backoffMs(attempt: number, random: number): number;     // BACKOFF_MS[attempt] ± 20 %
export function reduce(s: ChatState, f: ServerFrame): ChatState;        // pure
export class ChatClient {
  constructor(url: string, deps: ClientDeps, onChange: (s: ChatState) => void);
  readonly state: ChatState;
  open(): void; close(): void;                        // close() cancels timers; state keeps the messages
  send(body: string, lang?: string): string;          // returns the nonce
  retry(nonce: string): void;                         // re-sends with the SAME nonce (server is idempotent)
  report(id: number, category: ReportCategory): void; remove(id: number): void;
}
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { ChatClient, reduce, initialState, backoffMs, BACKOFF_MS, type SocketLike, type ChatState } from './chat-client';
import { ACK_TIMEOUT_MS } from './protocol';

class FakeSocket implements SocketLike {
  sent: string[] = []; closed?: number;
  onopen: SocketLike['onopen'] = null; onmessage: SocketLike['onmessage'] = null;
  onclose: SocketLike['onclose'] = null; onerror: SocketLike['onerror'] = null;
  send(d: string) { this.sent.push(d); }
  close(code = 1000) { this.closed = code; }
  frame(o: unknown) { this.onmessage?.({ data: JSON.stringify(o) }); }
}
function harness() {
  let t = 0; const timers: Array<{ at: number; fn: () => void; id: number }> = []; let n = 0;
  const sockets: FakeSocket[] = []; let state: ChatState = initialState;
  const client = new ChatClient('wss://chat.test/room/x', {
    connect: () => { const s = new FakeSocket(); sockets.push(s); return s; },
    now: () => t, random: () => 0.5, nonce: () => `nonce${String(++n).padStart(4, '0')}`,
    setTimer: (fn, ms) => { const id = ++n; timers.push({ at: t + ms, fn, id }); return id; },
    clearTimer: (id) => { const i = timers.findIndex((x) => x.id === id); if (i >= 0) timers.splice(i, 1); },
  }, (s) => { state = s; });
  const advance = (ms: number) => { t += ms; for (const x of timers.filter((y) => y.at <= t)) { timers.splice(timers.indexOf(x), 1); x.fn(); } };
  return { client, sockets, advance, get state() { return state; }, get timers() { return timers; } };
}
const hello = { t: 'hello', mode: 'open', history: [{ id: 1, ts: 1, handle: 'gh/a', body: 'old', state: 'visible' }], me: { handle: 'gh/me', canWrite: true, cooldownMs: 0 } };

describe('chat client', () => {
  let h: ReturnType<typeof harness>;
  beforeEach(() => { h = harness(); });
  it('opens nothing until open() is called', () => { expect(h.sockets).toHaveLength(0); expect(h.state.conn).toBe('idle'); });
  it('hello loads history and marks the room open', () => {
    h.client.open(); h.sockets[0].onopen?.(); h.sockets[0].frame(hello);
    expect(h.state).toMatchObject({ conn: 'open', loadedOnce: true, messages: [{ id: 1 }], me: { handle: 'gh/me' } });
  });
  it('a send without ack within ACK_TIMEOUT_MS becomes "failed", and retry re-uses the nonce', () => {
    h.client.open(); h.sockets[0].frame(hello);
    const nonce = h.client.send('hola', 'es');
    expect(h.state.pending).toEqual([{ nonce, body: 'hola', lang: 'es', status: 'sending' }]);
    h.advance(ACK_TIMEOUT_MS + 1);
    expect(h.state.pending[0].status).toBe('failed');
    h.client.retry(nonce);
    expect(JSON.parse(h.sockets[0].sent.at(-1)!)).toEqual({ t: 'send', body: 'hola', nonce, lang: 'es' });
    h.sockets[0].frame({ t: 'ack', nonce, id: 2, state: 'visible' });
    expect(h.state.pending).toEqual([]);
    expect(h.state.messages.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ id: 2, body: 'hola', mine: true, handle: 'gh/me' });
  });
  it('a held ack shows the message as held and mine', () => {
    h.client.open(); h.sockets[0].frame(hello);
    const nonce = h.client.send('mail me at x@y.org');
    h.sockets[0].frame({ t: 'ack', nonce, id: 3, state: 'held', holdCode: 'pii' });
    expect(h.state.messages.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ id: 3, state: 'held', holdCode: 'pii', mine: true });
  });
  it('reconnects with backoff, keeps what was loaded, and gives up visibly after the last step', () => {
    h.client.open(); h.sockets[0].frame(hello);
    h.sockets[0].onclose?.({ code: 1006 });
    expect(h.state).toMatchObject({ conn: 'retrying', messages: [{ id: 1 }] });
    for (let i = 0; i < BACKOFF_MS.length; i++) { h.advance(60_000); h.sockets.at(-1)!.onclose?.({ code: 1006 }); }
    expect(h.state.conn).toBe('failed');
    expect(h.state.messages).toHaveLength(1);        // "showing the last loaded", never "0 messages"
    expect(h.sockets).toHaveLength(BACKOFF_MS.length + 1);
  });
  it('pending sends are re-sent with the same nonce after a reconnect', () => {
    h.client.open(); h.sockets[0].frame(hello);
    const nonce = h.client.send('queued');
    h.sockets[0].onclose?.({ code: 1006 });
    h.advance(60_000); h.sockets[1].frame(hello);
    expect(h.sockets[1].sent.map((s) => JSON.parse(s).nonce)).toContain(nonce);
  });
  it('close 1008 (banned) does not reconnect', () => {
    h.client.open(); h.sockets[0].frame(hello); h.sockets[0].frame({ t: 'err', code: 'banned' });
    h.sockets[0].onclose?.({ code: 1008 }); h.advance(120_000);
    expect(h.sockets).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(h.state).toMatchObject({ conn: 'failed', lastError: 'banned' });
  });
  it('close() cancels every timer', () => {
    h.client.open(); h.sockets[0].frame(hello); h.client.send('x'); h.client.close();
    expect(h.timers).toHaveLength(0);
  });
});
describe('reduce', () => {
  it('remove blanks the body and keeps the statement of reasons', () => {
    const s = reduce({ ...initialState, messages: [{ id: 5, ts: 1, handle: 'gh/a', body: 'x', state: 'visible' }] }, { t: 'remove', id: 5, reason: 'rules §2 spam or flooding' });
    expect(s.messages[0]).toMatchObject({ body: '', state: 'removed', reason: 'rules §2 spam or flooding' });
  });
  it('an unknown frame is ignored, not applied', () => {
    expect(reduce(initialState, { t: 'nope' } as never)).toBe(initialState);
  });
  it('backoff has ±20 % jitter', () => {
    expect(backoffMs(0, 0)).toBe(800); expect(backoffMs(0, 1)).toBe(1200); expect(backoffMs(4, 0.5)).toBe(30_000);
  });
});
